Then, it localizes each concept through gradients of a presence score that contrasts the latent representations with their prototypes, producing masks that concentrate on the regions driving the concept. These gradients, propagated through a differentiable invertible mapping of the input such as a Fourier transform, yield localizations for the same concepts in the frequency domain. Finally, each concept receives a relevance score that quantifies its contribution to each class. On synthetic benchmarks, CENDRe achieves representation correctness comparable to state-of-the-art CE methods and significantly higher importance correctness. On real bearing-fault data, CENDRe extracts the frequency bands driving the model's predictions, located in regions commonly inspected for fault diagnosis, producing evidence to assess the model that time-domain CE methods cannot.

</details>


### [78] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Explainable Early Diagnosis of Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29530)
*Ranveer Singh,Pranuthi Tenali,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,Vaishali Phatak,Karla Lynch,Daniel Murman,Matthew Rizzo,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动化流水线，利用预训练基础模型从语言流畅性测试的音频录音中直接提取临床相关变量，构建贝叶斯网络（BN）来推理阿尔茨海默病（AD）进展标志物及其定性关系，成功恢复了已知临床知识并发现了新颖的语言标志物关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AD标志物识别依赖人工转录和专家分析，耗时费力且难以大规模开展，因此需要自动化方法来直接从音频中提取和推理AD进展指标。

Method: 使用预训练基础模型处理原始音频，提取临床相关变量，构建贝叶斯网络（BN），利用BN对AD进展标志物进行推理并推断各标记之间的定性关系。

Result: 该系统成功恢复了已知的临床知识，并识别出语言标志物之间新颖的关系。

Conclusion: 自动化流水线能够有效从音频中提取并推理AD进展标志物，既验证了已知临床知识，也发现了新的语言标志物关联，为大规模AD筛查和研究提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Identifying reliable Alzheimer's disease (AD) markers typically requires manual, labor-intensive transcription and expert analysis, limiting its scale. We introduce an automated pipeline that extracts qualitative knowledge about potential AD progression indicators directly from audio recordings of verbal fluency tests. Our method uses pretrained foundation models to process raw audio and extract clinically relevant variables to construct a Bayesian Network (BN); this BN is used to reason about the AD progression markers and infer their qualitative relationships. Our system successfully recovers known clinical knowledge and identifies novel relationships between linguistic markers.

</details>


### [79] [Pyramidal Width Can Increase Under Vertex Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29555)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文给出了Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi在2015年提出的关于多面体金字塔宽度（pyramidal width）猜想的精确反例。作者构造了R³中由六个整数顶点构成的多面体P和Q，其中Q通过向P添加一个顶点得到且P的所有顶点在Q中仍为顶点，但Q的金字塔宽度反而增大了约1.41倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi在2015年猜测：在多面体中添加一个顶点（且旧顶点仍保持为顶点）时，金字塔宽度不应增加。该猜想若成立，将有助于保证Frank-Wolfe算法中某些收敛性证明的有效性。本文旨在检验这一猜想是否正确。

Method: 作者构造了六个整数顶点v0至v5，定义P为前五个顶点的凸包，Q为全部六个顶点的凸包。利用金字塔宽度与面距离（facial distance）的等价性，通过整数支撑超平面证明两个多面体的面格（face lattice），并对每个面距离进行有限有理数计算来精确评估金字塔宽度。

Result: 所有P的五个顶点在Q中仍然保持为顶点，但P²的金字塔宽度为48/353，而Q²的金字塔宽度为36/133。因此顶点插入使金字塔宽度增大了√(1059/532)≈1.410886779倍，构成了该猜想的精确反例。论文还附带了无依赖的精确验证器。

Conclusion: 该反例推翻了Lacoste-Julien和Jaggi关于顶点插入不会增加金字塔宽度的猜想，表明通过添加顶点来增强多面体时，金字塔宽度（从而与Frank-Wolfe算法收敛相关的几何性质）可能发生退化。

Abstract: Lacoste-Julien and Jaggi conjectured in 2015 that the pyramidal width of a polytope cannot increase when a vertex is added, provided that every old point remains a vertex. We give an exact counterexample with six integer points in $\R^3$. For \[ P=\conv\{v_0,\ldots,v_4\},\qquad Q=\conv\{v_0,\ldots,v_5\}, \] where \[ \begin{aligned} v_0&=(-1,-3,-1), & v_1&=(3,2,-2), & v_2&=(0,2,1),\\ v_3&=(-1,-3,3), & v_4&=(-2,0,1), & v_5&=(-1,0,-2), \end{aligned} \] all five vertices of $P$ remain vertices of $Q$, but \[ \PWidth(P)^2=\frac{48}{353} \quad\text{and}\quad \PWidth(Q)^2=\frac{36}{133}. \] Thus vertex insertion increases pyramidal width by the factor $\sqrt{1059/532}\approx 1.410886779$. The proof uses the equivalence between pyramidal width and facial distance, certifies both face lattices by integer supporting hyperplanes, and evaluates every facial distance by a finite rational calculation. A dependency-free exact verifier accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [80] [Convergence and Regret of the Policy Gradient for Multi-Armed Bandits in Diffusion Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29593)
*Yanwei Jia,Du Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散环境下多臂老虎机问题的策略梯度更新，在连续时间强化学习框架下（SDE描述），采用logit参数化随机策略，证明了在任意常数学习率下算法几乎必然收敛到最优臂，并推导出步长低于时间不变阈值时的非渐近遗憾上界O(log T)，通过构造新Lyapunov函数改进了Lattimore(2026a)的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续时间强化学习框架（SDE）下，扩散环境中的多臂老虎机策略梯度算法的收敛性和遗憾界分析尚不完善，现有分析（如Lattimore 2026a）存在改进空间，需要更透明的理论工具来理解策略梯度的行为。

Method: 在Wang et al. (2020)和Jia & Zhou (2022b)的连续时间强化学习框架下，将多臂老虎机策略梯度更新建模为SDE。采用logit参数化随机策略，构造新颖的Lyapunov函数来证明几乎必然收敛性，并推导常数学习率下的非渐近遗憾上界。该Lyapunov函数同样适用于离散时间策略梯度算法的分析。

Result: 证明了在任意常数学习率下，策略梯度更新几乎必然收敛到最优臂；当常数学习率低于某个时间不变阈值时，获得O(log T)阶的非渐近遗憾上界，优于或改进了Lattimore (2026a)的分析结果。

Conclusion: 本文通过构造新颖的Lyapunov函数，展现了利用SDE工具分析策略梯度更新的透明性，不仅为连续时间情形提供了改进的理论保证，其Lyapunov方法还可推广至离散时间策略梯度分析，具有较强的方法论贡献。

Abstract: This paper studies the policy gradient update for a multi-arm bandit problem in diffusion environment that is described by a stochastic differential equation (SDE) under the continuous-time reinforcement learning framework by Wang et al. (2020), Jia and Zhou (2022b). With the logit parameterization for the stochastic policy, we show that it converges almost surely to the optimal arm under an arbitrary constant learning rate. Furthermore, we derive the non-asymptotic regret upper bound when the constant learning rate is below a time-invariant threshold; and the regret bound has order $O(\log T)$. We improve the analysis in Lattimore (2026a) for the same SDE by constructing a novel Lyapunov function and demonstrate the transparency of analyzing policy gradient using the tools in SDEs. In addition, the same Lyapunov function is also helpful in analyzing the discrete-time policy gradient algorithm.

</details>


### [81] [The Parts Are Greater Than the Sum: Automated Task Sequencing for Efficient Training of Multi-Policy LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29601)
*Jiajia Tang,Sizhe Yuen,Francisco Gomez Medina,Yali Du,Adam Sobey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大语言模型的参数高效微调（PEFT）的优化路径组织框架，通过任务分组与任务排序自动构建多条独立量化低秩适配器（QLoRA）优化路径，使异构任务在解耦的适配空间中优化，同时保持兼容任务间的正迁移。在TRACE基准上达到44.78的最佳性能，表明优化路径组织比单纯增加适配器容量更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PEFT使用单一共享低秩适配器（LoRA）进行适配，在处理异构任务序列时共享优化空间会产生严重的干扰，导致负迁移和灾难性遗忘。现有方法主要通过增加参数容量或组合多个适配器来提升表达能力，但本质上仍依赖共享的优化路径，未能从根本上解决异构任务间的优化冲突问题。

Method: 提出一种自动多策略PEFT架构，核心包括：(1) 在固定参数预算下，通过任务分组和任务排序自动组织优化兼容的适配路径；(2) 将组织好的优化路径实现为独立的量化低秩适配器（QLoRA）；(3) 异构任务在解耦的适配空间中被优化，同时兼容任务之间保持正迁移。

Result: 在TRACE基准上的实验表明，从传统单策略PEFT到多策略PEFT性能一致提升，所提出的自动多策略框架在相同可训练容量下达到最佳性能44.78。

Conclusion: 优化路径组织比单纯增加适配器容量对异构参数高效微调更为有效，通过任务分组与排序实现的多策略PEFT能显著缓解异构任务间的干扰问题。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) commonly adapts large language models using a single shared Low-Rank Adapter (LoRA). This shared optimization space often suffers from interference when adapting heterogeneous task sequences, leading to poor transfer and catastrophic forgetting. Existing approaches mainly improve adapter expressiveness by increasing parameter capacity or composing multiple adapters, yet they still rely on a shared optimization path. In this paper, we propose an optimization-path organization framework for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, implemented as an automatic multi-policy PEFT architecture. Specifically, optimization-compatible adaptation paths are automatically organized through task grouping and task sequencing under a fixed parameter budget. The organized optimization paths are implemented as independent Quantized Low-Rank Adapters (QLoRA), enabling heterogeneous tasks to be optimized in decoupled adaptation spaces while preserving positive transfer among compatible tasks. Experiments on the TRACE benchmark demonstrate that performance consistently improves from conventional single-policy PEFT to multi-policy PEFT, with the proposed automatic multi-policy framework achieving the best performance of 44.78 under the same trainable capacity. This suggests that optimization-path organization is more effective than simply increasing adapter capacity for heterogeneous parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [82] [Freeze, Then Select: Structured Field Adapters and Stability-Validated Weak Selection for PDE Discovery from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29665)
*Juncheng Zhong,Chenghuang Shen,Jianfeng Liu,Zhengdong Xiao,Longjiu Luo,Qianrong Wang,Wenjun Xu,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"冻结-选择"（freeze-then-select）方法，将非线性PDE发现中的方程选择与神经场重建解耦。通过结构化解适配器从稀疏观测中学习连续场，结合稳定性验证的弱选择（SVWS）在独立弱形式系统中识别候选项，最终在全部六个稀疏MDBench测试场景中取得最高的精确支持恢复率，并能从稀疏含噪数据中恢复未知非线性扩散函数的幂律形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在耦合神经PDE发现中，优化路径表现出三种行为：精确支撑集可能持续到训练结束、仅短暂出现或完全无法涌现。这表明方程选择与神经优化高度耦合，导致稀疏观测下的PDE发现不可靠。因此需要将方程选择从神经场优化中解耦出来。

Method: 方法包括两部分：(1) 结构化解适配器：从观测数据训练，无PDE残差约束，将场分解为学习到的空间特征与由三次样条表示的时间系数；(2) 稳定性验证弱选择（SVWS）：在冻结场之后，跨独立弱形式系统识别反复出现的项，重拟合候选支撑集，并在留出的弱形式系统上选择最终方程。此外，该方法还扩展至遗传编程生成的表达式库，用于恢复非线性扩散函数的幂律形式。

Result: 在全部六个稀疏MDBench测试场景中，该方法取得了最高的精确支持恢复率，在具有挑战性的Kuramoto-Sivashinsky动力学上相对于经典和神经基线提升最为显著。同时能够从稀疏含噪观测中恢复未知非线性扩散函数的幂律形式。

Conclusion: 将方程项选择与神经场优化解耦显著提升了稀疏观测下PDE发现的可靠性，且该方法不局限于固定库，可扩展至遗传编程生成的表达式，展现出对未知函数形式的发现能力。

Abstract: PDE discovery from sparse observations requires reconstructing a continuous field and selecting the correct differential terms. Our analysis of optimization paths in coupled neural PDE discovery reveals three behaviors: the exact support can persist to the end of training, appear only transiently, or fail to emerge. To decouple equation selection from neural optimization, we develop a freeze-then-select method combining a structured field adapter with Stability-Validated Weak Selection (SVWS). Trained from observations without a PDE residual, the adapter factorizes the field into learned spatial features and temporal coefficients represented by cubic splines. After freezing the field, SVWS identifies recurrent terms across independent weak-form systems, refits candidate supports, and selects the final equation on held-out weak-form systems. Beyond fixed libraries, we apply the same principle to expressions generated by genetic programming and recover the power-law form of an unknown nonlinear diffusion function from sparse, noisy observations. Across all six sparse MDBench regimes, our method attains the highest exact support recovery rate, with its clearest gains over classical and neural baselines on challenging Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamics.

</details>

### [83] [DeltaServe: Host-Agnostic Co-Serving of Inference and Fine-Tuning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28848)
*Jiaxuan Chen,Jianshu She,Ye Yuan,Rajat Ghosh,Karan Gupta,Qirong Ho,Xue Liu,Oana Balmau*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DeltaServe 是一种主机无关的协同服务设计，将 LLM 推理系统的空闲 GPU 算力转化为 LoRA 微调吞吐量，同时保持推理服务的 SLO 合规。它通过紧凑的钩子接口与现有推理引擎集成，利用推理预填充和 LoRA 微调前向传播的共享执行结构，并使用基于 CUDA 图感知延迟模型的 SLO 感知调度器来管理微调任务的准入和执行。在 vLLM、SGLang 和 S-LoRA 上集成后，在生产流量下实现了比 LLMStation 高 2.9 倍的微调吞吐量（100% SLO 合规对比 85%），且无需额外硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理系统为满足严格的延迟目标而按峰值负载配置资源，导致流量低于峰值时大量 GPU 计算资源闲置。作者希望利用这些空闲推理容量来执行 LoRA 微调任务，而无需额外硬件投入。

Method: 提出 DeltaServe，一种主机无关的协同服务架构：(1) 通过紧凑的钩子接口集成现有推理引擎，仅需支持多 LoRA 批处理；(2) 利用推理预填充与 LoRA 微调前向传播的共享执行结构；(3) 使用 SLO 感知调度器，仅在推理余量充足时准入和执行微调；(4) 调度器由离线校准、在线精化的 CUDA 图感知延迟模型驱动。

Result: 在 Company X 的生产流量追踪上，DeltaServe 集成到 vLLM 后，相比 LLMStation 实现 2.9 倍更高的微调吞吐量，100% 推理 SLO 合规（LLMStation 仅 85%）；相比 vLLM+torchtune 基线，微调吞吐量提升 39%，且无需额外硬件并保持完全 SLO 合规。系统已集成到 vLLM、SGLang 和 S-LoRA 三个引擎。

Conclusion: DeltaServe 证明了推理系统闲置的 GPU 容量可以被有效回收用于 LoRA 微调，在不牺牲推理 SLO 的前提下实现计算资源的高效复用，为 LLM 服务的资源利用率优化提供了可行的架构方案。

Abstract: LLM serving systems are provisioned for peak load to meet strict latency targets, leaving substantial GPU compute idle whenever traffic falls below peak. We present DeltaServe, a host-agnostic co-serving design that converts this idle inference capacity into LoRA fine-tuning throughput while preserving inference service-level objectives (SLOs). DeltaServe integrates with existing inference engines through a compact hook interface that requires only multi-LoRA batching support. It exploits the shared execution structure of inference prefill and LoRA fine-tuning forward passes, and uses an SLO-aware scheduler to admit and execute fine-tuning only when sufficient inference headroom is available. The scheduler is driven by a CUDA-graph-aware latency model calibrated offline and refined online. We integrate DeltaServe with vLLM, SGLang, and S-LoRA. On a production trace from Company X, DeltaServe on vLLM delivers 2.9x higher fine-tuning throughput than LLMStation at 100% inference SLO compliance, versus 85% for LLMStation. It also achieves 39% higher fine-tuning throughput than a baseline running vLLM+torchtune, using no additional hardware and maintaining full SLO compliance.

</details>


### [84] [LayoutBench: Performance Benchmarking of Cloud Storage Layouts for Multimedia Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28880)
*Debopam Sanyal,Hongjie Chen,Alexey Tumanov,Joshua Kimball*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LayoutBench是首个针对多媒体数据检索场景的存储布局基准测试工具，系统评估了三种存储布局策略（单个对象存储L1、tar归档L2、Parquet列式存储L3）在AWS S3上的检索性能、数据传输量和货币成本。实验表明L2在多数情况下延迟最低（依靠连接复用），L3在大规模检索时最快但数据传输量高且成本高出一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多媒体机器学习工作负载将大规模数据集存储在云对象存储服务（如AWS S3）中，样本的物理存储布局直接影响数据检索的速度和成本。然而现有基准测试主要针对数据库引擎和查询处理，没有系统评估不同存储布局在多媒体数据检索场景下的性能表现，导致存储决策缺乏可靠的实验依据。

Method: 提出LayoutBench基准框架，评估三种代表性存储布局策略：L1（每个样本单独存储为对象）、L2（样本顺序打包进tar归档文件）、L3（样本以列式组织存入Parquet文件）。使用ImageNet数据集，设计11种不同结果集大小的查询，在6种涵盖不同网络带宽和内存层级的AWS EC2实例配置上进行实验，测量检索时间、数据传输量和货币成本三个维度的指标。

Result: 实验发现：L2通过连接复用机制在检索延迟上优于L1和L3，但检索规模非常大时该优势消失；L3在超大规模检索中速度最快，但由于行组粒度的原因在所有查询规模下都传输了显著更多的数据，且内存需求更高；在所有布局中，数据传输成本占总支出主导地位，L3的总成本比L1和L2高出一个数量级。

Conclusion: 不同存储布局在多媒体数据检索中各有优劣：L2适合一般规模检索，L3适合大规模检索但需权衡高额数据传输成本和内存开销，L1则提供灵活性和较低成本但延迟较高。数据转移成本是一个关键影响因素，存储布局的选择应综合考虑查询规模、网络带宽和成本预算。

Abstract: Modern multimedia machine learning workloads increasingly store large-scale datasets in cloud object storage services such as AWS S3. How these samples are physically organized in storage (i.e.,storage layout) directly affects how quickly and cheaply they can be retrieved. Yet the benchmarks used to guide storage decisions today focus on database engines and query processing, and none systematically evaluates how different storage layouts perform for multimedia data retrieval. We present LayoutBench, the first benchmark designed to fill this gap. It evaluates three representative layout strategies: storing each sample as an individual object (L1), sequentially packing samples into tar archives (L2), and organizing samples as columns in Parquet files (L3). We measure retrieval time, data transferred, and monetary cost using 11 queries of varying result-set sizes on ImageNet across six AWS EC2 instance configurations that span different network bandwidth and memory tiers. Our experiments reveal that L2 achieves lower latency than L1 and L3 through connection reuse, but loses this advantage as retrieval sizes become very large. L3 is the fastest for very large retrievals but transfers substantially more data across all query sizes due to row-group granularity, and requires significantly more memory. Across all layouts, data transfer cost dominates total expenditure, with L3 costing an order of magnitude more than L1 or L2.

</details>


### [85] [Rethinking AI Cloud Infrastructure for Agentic Serving Systems with the Aries Experimentation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29069)
*Leonid Kondrashov,Hongrui Liu,JooYoung Park,Boxi Zhou,Zonghao Liu,Chengzhi Lu,Riccardo Mancini,Esha Choukse,Haris Javaid,German Sviridov,Tao Peng,Chen Zhao,Anastasia Avdeeva,Aleksei Gusev,Marios Kogias,Luo Mai,Dmitrii Ustiugov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Aries，一个用于自主AI代理（autonomous agents）的全栈实验框架，可分离任务语义与执行配置、重建跨组件代理轨迹、并通过统一接口支持异构沙箱环境下的有状态工具执行。基于开放基准与生产环境跟踪，揭示token中心指标忽略非推理瓶颈、保留额外上下文收益递减且降低服务容量、工具沙箱长空闲与短突发交替等关键发现，并提出代理原生服务系统的愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理对传统LLM服务构成挑战，因为其将重复推理与持久上下文和沙箱工具执行耦合在一起，现有系统缺乏对这类工作负载的系统性实验框架与生产级洞察。

Method: 构建Aries全栈实验框架，包含三个核心能力：(1) 将任务语义与执行配置解耦；(2) 通过相关性系统遥测重建跨组件代理轨迹；(3) 在不同异构沙箱子系统上提供统一的有状态工具执行接口。使用开放代理基准与商业平台生产轨迹进行可复现实验。

Result: 三项关键发现：(1) token为中心的指标无法捕捉非推理瓶颈；(2) 保留更多上下文提升准确性的边际收益递减，同时降低服务容量；(3) 工具沙箱呈现长空闲与短资源突发交替的模式，而基于快照的状态管理使激进挂起代价高昂。安全分析进一步表明需要缩小沙箱攻击面。

Conclusion: 提出代理原生（agent-native）服务系统的愿景，围绕轨迹级指标、自适应上下文管理、弹性沙箱资源管理以及最小化攻击面的沙箱进行设计。

Abstract: Autonomous agents challenge conventional LLM serving by coupling repeated inference with persistent context and sandboxed tool execution. We present Aries, a full-stack experimentation framework that separates task semantics from execution configurations, reconstructs cross-component agent trajectories with correlated system telemetry, and exposes stateful tool execution through a consistent interface across heterogeneous sandbox substrates. We use Aries to conduct reproducible experiments on open agent harnesses and benchmarks. We complement these experiments with production traces from a commercial platform, grounding low-level systems research in observed production behavior. Our results show that (1) token-centric metrics miss non-inference bottlenecks, (2) retaining additional context yields diminishing accuracy benefits while reducing serving capacity, and (3) tool sandboxes alternate between long idle periods and short resource bursts, while current snapshot-based state management makes aggressive suspension costly. A complementary security analysis further highlights the need to reduce the sandbox attack surface. We then discuss the vision for agent-native serving systems designed around trajectory-level metrics, adaptive context management, elastic sandbox resource management, and sandboxes with minimized attack surface.

</details>


### [86] [Knox: Fortifying Smart Spaces With Safety Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29198)
*Rishabh Menezes,Jadon T. Schuler,Kaimeng Zhu,Oliver Rogalski,Indranil Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Knox是首个面向IoT智能空间的安全检查系统，通过新的安全规范语言和两种静态安全检查算法，在NP-hard问题背景下显著降低检查时间并保持高精度捕获安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能空间和建筑中的IoT设备是新兴的分布式系统类别，具有关键的安全需求，但此前缺乏系统性的安全检查方法。

Method: (i) 提出一种用于智能空间安全子句的新规范语言；(ii) 提出了两种新的静态验证算法，用于验证智能空间中多个例程的多个安全属性。由于该问题是NP-hard的，作者设计了新颖且可解释的算法，并提出了进一步降低运行时的优化技术。

Result: 通过真实数据集的分析和实验，Knox相比基线方法显著减少了检查时间，同时在捕获安全违规方面保持了高准确性。

Conclusion: Knox证明了在IoT智能空间中实现高效且准确的静态安全检查是可行的，为该领域提供了首个系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) devices in smart spaces and buildings are an emerging class of distributed systems with critical safety requirements. This paper presents Knox, the first system to enable safety checking in IoT-enabled smart spaces. Knox's contributions include (i) safety specifications: a new language for safety clauses in such smart spaces, and (ii) static safety checking: two new algorithms for static verification of multiple safety properties across multiple routines running inside a smart space. Since the latter problem is NP-hard, we present and analyze novel and explainable algorithms for the static version of the problem. We also present optimizations that further reduce runtime. Our analysis and experimental results with real datasets show that Knox reduces checking time significantly compared to baselines, while providing high accuracy in catching safety violations.

</details>


### [87] [Allocation Tracking and Parameter Checking for Parallel Programming Models using Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29303)
*Yussur Mustafa Oraji,Christian Bischof*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoVer框架合约语言的扩展，引入通用参数检查和分配追踪机制，以支持更广泛的错误类型验证，同时保持跨编程模型和语言的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算程序的正确性检查工具通常局限于特定的并行编程模型（如MPI或OpenSHMEM）。CoVer框架此前通过通用合约方法解耦了API需求与核心工具，但其有效性受限于底层合约语言的表达能力，无法覆盖更多类型的错误，需要扩展合约语言以捕获和检查更广泛的错误类别。

Method: 扩展CoVer合约语言，引入两项新特性：（1）通用参数检查（generic parameter checking），用于验证更广泛范围内的参数错误；（2）分配追踪（allocation tracking），用于跟踪和管理内存分配相关的错误。这些扩展保持了跨编程模型和语言的通用性。

Result: 扩展后的CoVer在多个语言上保持了分析准确性的一致性，验证了框架的通用适用性。虽然额外的运行时分析自然引入了性能开销，但准确率有显著提升。

Conclusion: CoVer合约语言的扩展大幅增强了其工具实用性，在引入可接受的性能开销前提下实现了显著的准确率提升，同时维持了跨语言和跨编程模型的通用性。

Abstract: Correctness checking tools for High-Performance Computing programs are typically limited to specific parallel programming models such as MPI or OpenSHMEM. The CoVer framework previously addressed this by introducing a generic, contract-based approach that decoupled API requirements from the core tool. However, CoVer's effectiveness remains bounded by the expressiveness of its underlying contract language, restricting the types of errors it can verify. This paper presents an extension to the CoVer contract language designed to capture and check a broader range of error classes. Our extensions introduce generic parameter checking and allocation tracking, while keeping generality across both programming model and language. We evaluate these extensions and demonstrate that analysis accuracy remains consistent across multiple languages, reinforcing the framework's general applicability. While the additional runtime analyses naturally incur a performance overhead, these improvements greatly enhance CoVer's utility with a significant accuracy improvement.

</details>


### [88] [CARA: Exact Local Repair with Fresh One-Action Certification for Cloud Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29465)
*Xiyang Zhang,Yuanhe Tian,Hongzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CARA（Certificate-Aligned Recomposition），一种基于模拟器的局部修复管线，将自适应提议生成与一次性部署决策分离，在有界两主机邻域内通过装箱特定可容许界恢复精确的top-P不同动作，并在保留视图上使用新配对认证进行选择，从而在无需依赖上游搜索规模的前提下，将四向改进的误报概率控制在0.05以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模拟器的修复流水线会检查大量修复方案，但只有在多个可靠性指标同时改善时才部署。然而，重用搜索场景来测试所选动作会破坏名义证据的有效性，而标量化方法可能牺牲最弱的标准。因此需要一种能同时保证认证有效性与选择鲁棒性的方法。

Method: CARA采用驻留锚定（incumbent-anchored）管线，将自适应提议生成与一次性部署决策分离。在有界两主机邻域中，利用装箱特定的可容许界在混合成对二元/固定赌注证书顺序下恢复精确的top-P不同动作；随后使用保留视图选择单一动作并提交其投注计划，再通过新鲜配对认证（fresh paired Certification）进行评估。该方法在符号独立性与条件矩假设下收敛。

Result: 在前瞻性冻结研究中，覆盖128个独立合成环境和四个重复上下文，完整的故障关闭终端策略在所有环境中对所有五个评估端点均改善了开发选定的同主机计数驻留方案。J下降3.57个百分点，连续负担下降21-28%。匹配排序敏感性分析产生低于5.3×10⁻⁴的绝对平均差距，未确立排序优越性。

Conclusion: CARA将精确可审计的提议恢复与选择鲁棒的鲜认证相结合，为最后一英里的局部修复提供了可靠保障，在不依赖上游搜索规模或复杂性的前提下保证误报概率上限，并通过实证验证了其在多评估端点上的全面改善效果。

Abstract: Simulator-based placement pipelines may inspect many repairs but deploy only when several reliability criteria improve together. Reusing search scenes to test the selected action invalidates nominal evidence, while scalarization can trade away the weakest criterion. We introduce Certificate-Aligned Recomposition (CARA), an incumbent-anchored pipeline that separates adaptive proposal generation from a one-use deployment decision. In a bounded two-host neighborhood, a packing-specific admissible bound recovers the exact top-$P$ distinct actions under a mixed paired-binary/fixed-bet certificate order. Held-out views then select one action and commit its betting plans before fresh paired Certification opens. Under the stated sign-independence and conditional-moment assumptions, the probability of falsely declaring four-way improvement is at most $.05$, irrespective of the size or complexity of upstream search. In a prospectively frozen study over 128 independent synthetic environments and four repeated contexts, the complete fail-closed terminal policy improved a development-selected same-host-count incumbent on all five Evaluation endpoints in every environment. $J$ fell by 3.57 percentage points and the continuous burdens by 21--28%. A matched ordering sensitivity produced absolute mean gaps below $5.3\times10^{-4}$ and does not establish order superiority. CARA thus couples exact auditable proposal recovery to selection-robust fresh certification for last-mile local repair.

</details>


### [89] [System-Wide Termination in Distributed Betweenness Centrality Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29474)
*Siamak Abdi,Lucia Cavallaro,Giuseppe Di Fatta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于分布式介数中心性计算的轻量级系统级全局终止检测算法，使各顶点能本地判断全局是否收敛，避免因独立本地停止导致的过早终止或多余消息传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式介数中心性实现缺乏轻量级的系统级终止检测机制，可能导致收敛后产生额外消息传输，或在配置不当时提前停止。

Method: 提出系统级全局终止检测算法，使顶点能本地决定整个系统是否收敛；并通过自定义Python模拟器，在合成网络（Erdos-Renyi、几何网络）和真实网络（Email、Road）上，将该方法与传统本地停止策略进行对比评估。

Result: 系统级终止检测使顶点在检测到全局收敛后安全停止（在评估网络中最终误差为零）；而本地停止策略在异质网络上会导致过早终止并产生一定误差。

Conclusion: 分布式中心性计算中需要协调一致的终止机制，系统级全局终止检测优于独立的本地停止策略。

Abstract: Computing betweenness centrality on large networks is inherently expensive, as it requires aggregating shortest-path dependencies across all pairs of vertices and becomes increasingly difficult to scale as network size grows. Scalable distributed algorithms can facilitate such computations, particularly when centralised processing is not feasible, and message exchanges must be carefully controlled, for example, in bandwidth-limited or very large-scale networks. However, existing distributed betweenness centrality implementations do not integrate a lightweight, system-wide termination detector. As a consequence, this can lead to extra messaging after local convergence or, if misconfigured, premature stops. In this work, a lightweight, system-wide global termination detection algorithm for this task is presented. The proposed method enables vertices to decide locally when the overall system has converged. The method is evaluated against a local stopping strategy in which vertices terminate individually once their own estimates stabilise. To compare these two approaches, namely global termination detection and local stopping, a custom Python simulator is implemented, and both approaches are tested on synthetic (Erdos-Renyi and geometric) and real (Email and Road) network topologies. Our results show that system-wide termination detection lets vertices stop safely after detecting global convergence, as indicated by zero final error in the evaluated networks, rather than stopping independently based only on local convergence. The local stopping approach, on the other hand, results in premature termination and some errors on heterogeneous networks. This work emphasises the need for coordinated halting in distributed centrality computation.

</details>


### [90] [SLIM: Saturation-Aware Lightweight Performance Modeling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29575)
*Pol G. Recasens,Ferran Agullo,Yue Zhu,Chen Wang,Jordi Torres,Josep Ll. Berral*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过GPU硬件剖析技术揭示LLM推理吞吐量饱和的根本原因在于解码阶段注意力核的DRAM带宽饱和（而非简单的批次增大），并提出BCA（批处理配置顾问）和SLIM（半解析性能模型）来选择最优批处理配置并预测吞吐与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中增大批处理大小虽能提升吞吐量，但达到某个平台期后收益边际递减且增加延迟和GPU内存消耗。以往研究将这一现象归因于HBM/DRAM带宽限制，但缺乏系统性的硬件级验证。作者希望通过精细的GPU剖析来揭示真正的根因，并提供实用的性能优化工具。

Method: 1) 使用硬件级剖析技术对GPU进行详细表征，定位吞吐量饱和发生于解码阶段的注意力核。2) 证明驱动DRAM带宽饱和的关键因素是活动上下文长度增长下注意力核近恒定的算术强度，而非仅仅批次变大；同时计算吞吐远低于硬件上限。3) 提出BCA框架，在满足目标延迟约束下选择最高吞吐量的批处理配置，并可避免最多55 GB的GPU内存分配。4) 引入SLIM半解析性能模型，基于Transformer计算量和内存流量的解析公式预测推理吞吐与延迟。

Result: 在评估的OPT模型上，BCA在极小吞吐损失下识别出最多可节省55 GB的GPU内存分配；SLIM在多个场景下优于代表性性能建模基线，并能成功泛化到未见过的运行条件。

Conclusion: LLM推理吞吐量饱和的根因是解码阶段注意力核的DRAM带宽饱和（由活动上下文长度增长导致的恒定算术强度驱动），而非硬件计算能力限制。基于这一洞察，BCA和SLIM提供了数据驱动的批处理配置优化与性能预测解决方案，可有效兼顾吞吐量、延迟和内存效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving commonly increases batch size to improve throughput, but performance eventually reaches a deployment-dependent plateau beyond which larger batches provide marginal gains while increasing latency and GPU memory consumption. Previous studies have attributed this behavior to HBM/DRAM bandwidth limitations, but the underlying causes have primarily been supported by conceptual arguments or high-level performance observations. As our first contribution, we present a detailed GPU characterization using hardware profiling techniques, demonstrating that throughput saturation originates in the attention kernels during the decode phase. Specifically, we show that their nearly constant arithmetic intensity as active-context lengths increases -not merely larger batch sizes- drives DRAM-bandwidth saturation, while the achieved compute throughput remains far below the hardware limit. Building on this analysis, we present the Batching Configuration Advisor (BCA), which selects the highest-throughput batching configuration satisfying a target latency constraint and identifies up to 55 GB of GPU memory allocation that can be avoided for the evaluated OPT models with minimal throughput loss. To enable these recommendations, we introduce SLIM (Saturation-Aware Lightweight Performance Model), a semi-analytical model that predicts LLM inference throughput and latency from analytical formulations of Transformer computation and memory traffic. Across the evaluated scenarios, SLIM outperforms representative performance-modeling baselines while successfully generalizing to previously unseen operating conditions.

</details>

### [91] [OpenClaw and Ollama in Agentic AI: Toward Fully Autonomous and Scalable AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28629)
*Konstantinos I. Roumeliotis,Ranjan Sapkota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Agentic AI的分层架构（推理层、编排层、执行层分离），并以OpenClaw-Ollama作为全栈系统进行实验验证，证明持久记忆、工具使用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成而非单一模型，同时讨论了可扩展性、安全性、隐私、治理和评估等挑战，为构建可扩展、安全、可信的自主智能体系统提供路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从被动式大语言模型向持久、可执行能力的系统快速转变，暴露了Agentic AI架构理解上的关键缺口，特别是在推理、编排和执行层分离方面。尽管近期有诸多进展，但统一的全栈智能体系统设计与评估框架仍然有限，亟需建立统一的架构基础。

Method: 提出一种全面的分层架构，将Agentic AI从反应式LLM接口演化为具备记忆、规划和持续执行的持久目标驱动型自主智能体；以Ollama作为LLM推理层、OpenClaw作为代理运行时编排层的OpenClaw-Ollama全栈系统进行原型实验验证，分析能力涌现与系统集成、架构复杂度之间的关系。

Result: 实验验证表明持久记忆、工具利用和自适应决策等能力源自系统级集成而非独立模型的效果，且性能随架构复杂度增加而持续提升；同时识别了可扩展性、安全性、隐私、治理和评估方面的挑战，强调需要稳健的基准测试和系统级设计。

Conclusion: 该工作为Agentic AI建立了统一的架构基础，验证了全栈自主AI智能体的有效性，并为构建可扩展、安全、可信的智能体系统提供了路线图。未来方向包括可扩展多智能体架构、分布式自主系统及面向负责任部署的人本Agentic AI框架，相关模型、代码和数据集均已公开以支持复现和基准测试。

Abstract: The rapid transition from reactive large language models (LLMs) to persistent, action-capable systems has exposed critical gaps in the architectural understanding of Agentic AI, particularly in separating inference, orchestration, and execution layers for autonomous AI agents. Despite recent advances, unified frameworks for designing and evaluating full-stack agentic systems remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive, layered architecture for Agentic AI, outlining the evolution from reactive LLM interfaces to persistent, goal-driven autonomous AI agents with memory, planning, and continuous execution. We analyze OpenClaw and Ollama as a full-stack Agentic AI system, where Ollama serves as the LLM inference layer and OpenClaw enables agent runtime orchestration, integrating reasoning, tool use, and action execution. A prototype experimental validation of the OpenClaw-Ollama architecture demonstrates that capabilities such as persistent memory, tool utilization, and adaptive decision-making emerge from system-level integration rather than standalone models, with performance improving consistently as architectural complexity increases. The study further examines challenges in scalability, security, privacy, governance, and evaluation of agentic systems, highlighting the need for robust benchmarking and system-level design. Future directions include scalable multi-agent architectures, distributed autonomous systems, and human-aware Agentic AI frameworks for responsible deployment. Overall, this work establishes a unified architectural foundation for Agentic AI, validates the effectiveness of full-stack autonomous AI agents, and provides a roadmap for building scalable, secure, and trustworthy agentic systems. All models, code, and datasets are publicly released to support reproducibility and benchmarking.

</details>


### [92] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一种基于自动化同行评审的AI科学家系统评估基准协议，利用前沿LLM从原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个维度评估论文。对四个主流AI科学家框架（Sakana AI v1/v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper）生成的60篇论文与FARS公司的15篇基准论文进行了评估，发现FARS论文显著优于其他系统（平均分2.14-2.47 vs 1.00-1.87），验证了多模型LLM评估的可靠性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI科学家系统有望加速科学发现，但缺乏严格客观的方法来评估和比较AI生成论文的质量，这是当前该领域面临的关键开放性挑战。

Method: 利用三个前沿LLM（GPT-5.4、Gemini、Claude）作为独立评审者，构建自动化同行评审系统，从原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个维度评估论文。在一致的15个研究提案上运行四个AI科学家框架，生成60篇论文，并与15篇FARS基准论文一同评估。

Result: FARS基准论文在所有评估维度上显著超过其他框架（平均分2.14-2.47 vs 1.00-1.87），在Gemini和Claude评估中分数是次优系统的2倍以上。Gemini与Claude高度一致（ρ=0.907），且与综合评分极强相关（ρ=0.961），验证了评估可靠性；但GPT-5.4与其他评估者一致性较弱（ρ≈0.32）。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了首个AI科学家系统的定量基准，证明了多模型LLM评估为评估自主研究质量提供了可扩展、一致的框架，同时也揭示了不同LLM评审标准可能存在差异。

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [93] [LLM Framework for Discovering Major Mathematical Conjectures: AI's Quest for the Next Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28632)
*Alizer Wong,Zixin Zeng,Yi Tan,Wenyuan Li,Xuhang Chen,Xingru Lai,Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Yanhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段流水线用于重大数学猜想的系统性发现与验证，包括区域搜索、反思性验证和Lean 4/Mathlib形式化验证。实验显示20个候选全部通过Lean解析与类型检查，且无重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大数学猜想仍高度依赖专家直觉，目前缺乏统一的系统化方法来生成和验证具有实质数学潜力的猜想，因此需要一种自动化的猜想发现与验证流程。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 区域搜索（region search），从显式局部证据模块出发寻找候选猜想；(2) 反思性验证（reflective validation），评估猜想的基础性、新颖性和潜在重要性；(3) 形式化验证（formal validation），在Lean 4和Mathlib中进行形式化验证。

Result: 对20个候选进行实验：全部20个通过Lean解析和类型检查；全部20个不被exact?直接吸收；全部20个不被aesop自动消解；且无显式重复或近似重复。

Conclusion: 该方法能够稳定地从自然语言管道过渡到形式化检查，发现具有高"题目品味"（problem taste）的数学问题，其证明可能重组研究领域的语言并为人类数学研究提供持久帮助。

Abstract: Major mathematical conjectures still depend heavily on expert intuition, so a unified method for the systematic generation and validation of conjectures with substantial mathematical potential remains unavailable. We present a three stage pipeline for major conjecture discovery, with region search from explicit local evidence modules, reflective validation for foundationality, novelty, and potential significance, and formal validation in Lean 4 and Mathlib. The objective is the discovery of mathematical problems with high problem taste, namely problems whose proofs could reorganize the language of a research area and provide durable help to human mathematical research. Experiments on twenty candidates showstable passage from natural language to formal checks, with twenty out of twenty candidates passing Lean parsing and type checking, twenty out of twenty candidates not directly absorbed by exact?,twenty out of twenty candidates not automatically discharged by aesop, and no explicit duplicates or near duplicates.

</details>


### [94] [ThinkReset: Learnable Intermediate Interface Construction for Bounded-Context Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28642)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThinkReset框架，主张在有限上下文窗口下，长链推理的核心瓶颈并非轨迹压缩或测试时控制，而是缺乏可复用的中间接口来替代已丢弃的历史信息并支持持续求解。ThinkReset通过接口写回与重置机制构建可复用的中间接口，并直接优化重置后继续求解的成功率，在多个长时程推理基准上一致提升了固定上下文窗口下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维推理虽然能提升复杂问题的性能，但引入了冗余累积、上下文溢出和错误锚定等问题。作者认为在受限上下文窗口下，核心瓶颈不是轨迹压缩或测试时控制，而是缺少一个可复用的中间接口来替代被丢弃的历史信息并支持持续求解。同时，作者识别出结果奖励驱动的长链强化学习的一个关键失败模式：当模型在窗口即将耗尽前仍未解决问题时，最终答案奖励会鼓励模型过早猜测而非继续谨慎推理。

Method: ThinkReset是这一观点的文本空间实例化。它通过接口写回（interface writeback）和重置（reset）显式构建可复用的中间接口，并直接优化重置后继续求解的成功率。具体而言，该方法在推理过程中主动将中间状态与关键信息写入接口，并在上下文窗口受限时重置推理轨迹，利用已构建的接口替代被丢弃的历史信息继续求解。

Result: 在多个长时程推理基准上，ThinkReset在固定上下文窗口下一致地提升了任务成功率，验证了可复用中间接口相对于轨迹压缩和测试时控制的优势。

Conclusion: 在有限上下文窗口下，为长链推理构建可复用的中间接口比压缩轨迹或控制测试时过程更为有效。ThinkReset通过接口写回与重置机制，并针对重置后继续求解的成功率进行直接优化，能够有效缓解结果奖励驱动的过早猜测问题，从而在固定上下文窗口下显著提升长时程推理性能。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves performance on complex problems, but it also introduces redundancy accumulation, context overflow, and error anchoring. We argue that under bounded context windows, the core bottleneck is not trajectory compression or test-time control, but the absence of a reusable intermediate interface that can replace discarded history and support continued solving. We further identify a key failure mode of outcome-reward-driven long-chain reinforcement learning: when the model has not solved the task before the window is nearly exhausted, the final-answer reward encourages premature guessing rather than continued careful reasoning. We propose ThinkReset, a text-space instantiation of this view. ThinkReset explicitly constructs reusable intermediate interfaces through interface writeback and reset, and directly optimizes post-reset continuation success. Across multiple long-horizon reasoning benchmarks, this perspective consistently improves success rates under fixed context windows.

</details>


### [95] [TAPR: Enhancing LLM Performance with a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28657)
*Oliver Savolainen,Emanuele Bastianelli,Hosein Azarbonyad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TAPR（Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter），一个通过强化学习训练的任务感知提示词重写模型，利用GRPO算法和LLM-as-judge评估信号，将用户原始提示词重写为任务优化提示词，从而提升下游LLM性能。在问答、摘要和算术推理等任务上均取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常需要精心设计的提示词才能发挥全部潜力，但这对非专业用户构成了使用障碍。现有方法缺乏对特定任务的自适应优化能力，需要一种能够自动将用户提示词转化为任务优化形式的解决方案。

Method: 提出TAPR模型，基于Phi-4-mini-instruct微调。采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)进行强化学习训练，奖励信号来自LLM-as-judge对重写后的提示词和对应任务输出的双重评估。模型学习将原始提示词重写为更清晰、更具指导性的任务优化提示词。

Result: 在问答、摘要、算术推理等多样化任务上，TAPR相对于基础模型在提示词重写能力上取得一致提升。在Natural Questions和GSM8K等基准上获得更高准确率，重写后的提示词包含更清晰、更具指导性的语言。

Conclusion: TAPR有效解决了非专业用户提示词工程困难的问题，通过RL训练使模型能够生成任务优化的提示词从而提升下游LLM性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often require carefully crafted prompts to unlock their full potential, which can be a barrier for non-expert users. This work addresses the challenge by introducing a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR), a model that reformulates user prompts into task-optimized prompts with the explicit goal of improving downstream LLM performance. We train TAPR using reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where rewards are derived from LLM-as-judge evaluations of both the reformulated prompt and the corresponding task output. Experimental results on diverse tasks, such as question answering, summarization, and arithmetic reasoning, show that our method yields consistent gains over base models in prompt rewriting ability. Fine-tuning Phi-4-mini-instruct (as the base model for TAPR) produces prompts that contain clearer and more instructive language, leading to higher accuracy on established benchmarks such as Natural Questions and GSM8K. Our code is available at: https://github.com/OliverSavolainen/task-specific-prompt-rewriter

</details>


### [96] [Empowering Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation with Hybrid Tokenization and Serial-Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28659)
*Yuxuan Hu,Yuhao Wang,Tianbo Huang,Chao Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Lihua Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenCDSR 提出一个高效有效的跨域序列推荐生成式框架，通过跨域混合分词机制和多塔架构捕获跨域共性及领域特异性，并设计跨域串并行解码策略大幅降低推理延迟，在三个公开数据集上平均准确率提升1.5%，推理延迟降低85.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域序列推荐方法存在两个关键问题：(1) 生成式推荐中基于语义标识符的分词过程忽略了跨域协作相关性；(2) 采用beam search等低效解码策略，推理开销大，难以满足实时部署需求。

Method: (1) 提出跨域混合分词机制，采用多塔架构，通过层次化的共享-特定（shared-specific）和细粒度码本来联合捕获跨域共性与领域差异；(2) 设计跨域串并行解码策略，利用层次化语义标识符结构对生成过程进行部分并行化，在保持生成一致性的同时显著降低推理延迟。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，GenCDSR相比最先进的基线方法，平均准确率提升1.5%，平均推理延迟降低85.1%。

Conclusion: GenCDSR通过共享-特定码本和串并行解码策略，同时解决了跨域协作信息利用不足和生成推理效率低下的问题，验证了生成式框架在跨域序列推荐中的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation (CDSR) aims to model users' dynamic interest transitions and sequential patterns across multiple domains. Recently, generative recommendation (GR) has emerged. It first learns semantic identifiers (SIDs) from item semantics and formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation. However, existing methods face two critical issues: (1) they ignore collaborative correlations across domains during tokenization, and (2) they adopt inefficient decoding strategies, such as beam search, during generation, which hinders real-time deployment. To address these limitations, we propose GenCDSR, an effective and efficient generative framework for CDSR. Specifically, we design a cross-domain hybrid tokenization mechanism with a multi-tower architecture to jointly capture cross-domain commonalities and domain-specific distinctions through hierarchical shared-specific and fine-grained codebooks. Furthermore, we develop a cross-domain serial-parallel decoding strategy that leverages the hierarchical SID structure to partially parallelize generation, significantly reducing inference latency while preserving generation consistency. Experiments on three public datasets show that GenCDSR achieves an average accuracy improvement of 1.5 percent and an average inference latency reduction of 85.1 percent compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code and datasets are available online: https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/RecSys2026_GenCDSR.

</details>


### [97] [An Ontology-Guided, Deduplication-Aware Extraction Layer for Knowledge Graph Construction from Heterogeneous Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28662)
*Vaibhav Dangaich,Kevin Lewis,Kundeshwar Pundalik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个生产级知识图谱抽取系统，该系统利用本地Qwen3.5-9B模型，通过本体引导式抽取（ontologu-guided extraction）将实时文档流转换为符合正式本体的验证知识图谱，并经过五阶段精炼管线将搜索召回率从70%提升至95%，同时无错误合并并修复了七类静默质量缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在从非结构化文档中提取实体与关系时虽然流畅但在一致性方面存在严重问题：类型词汇表在不同文档间碎片化、同一人物以多种姓名变体出现、关系重复、以及同名不同人的静默混淆。这些问题导致生成的知识图谱质量不可靠，无法用于生产环境。

Method: 系统架构包括：1) 从Kafka消费文档元数据，通过为PDF、电子表格、Office和图像格式定制的处理器路由内容；2) 使用基于本体微调的本地Qwen3.5-9B模型进行两轮实体与关系提取；3) 核心创新为"本体引导式抽取"：按嵌入相似度实时从图数据库中检索本体相关切片并注入提示词，相较静态领域切片降低约94%目录开销；4) 五阶段精炼管线：确定性清洗、跨块合并、第二轮关系提取、六个无需模型推理的去重算法、以及嵌入消解子系统（其冲突守卫不允许任何相似度分数覆盖）。

Result: 在情报语料上的评估显示，搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%，且零错误合并；同时修复了七类静默质量缺陷，包括导致源文本被截断一个字符的bug，以及携带标题前缀的实体被系统性重复的问题。

Conclusion: 通过本体引导与确定性精炼管线的结合，可以在生产环境中实现既流畅又一致的LLM知识抽取，兼顾了模型的灵活性与规则的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models extract entities and relationships from unstructured documents fluently but inconsistently: type vocabularies fracture across documents, the same person surfaces under several name variants, relationships duplicate, and distinct individuals who share a name risk silent conflation. This paper presents the design, implementation, and empirical refinement of a production extraction layer that converts a live document stream into a validated knowledge graph aligned to a formal ontology. The system consumes document metadata from Kafka, routes PDF, spreadsheet, Office, and image content through handlers built for each format, and extracts entities and relationships in two passes using a locally hosted Qwen3.5-9B model tuned on the ontology. Its distinguishing component is ontology-guided extraction: the relevant slice of a curated ontology is retrieved live from a graph database by embedding similarity and injected into the extraction prompt, reducing catalog overhead by about 94 percent relative to static domain slices. Extracted results then pass through a refinement pipeline of five stages: deterministic cleaning, merging across chunks, a second pass for relationships, six deduplication algorithms that require no model inference, and an embedding resolution subsystem whose conflict guard no similarity score can override. Evaluation on intelligence corpora improved search recall from roughly 70 to 95 percent with no false merges, and corrected seven classes of silent quality defect, ranging from a bug that truncated source text by a single character to the systematic duplication of entities that carried title prefixes.

</details>


### [98] [How Hard Does It Think? Analyzing Step-Aware Reasoning Energy in LLM Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28674)
*Hui Wei,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Sizhe Zhou,Yizhu Jiao,Ming Zhong,Bowen Jin,Tong Yu,Shijia Pan,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SARE（步骤感知推理能量）框架，通过CKA核对齐度量相邻Transformer层间token隐藏状态Gram矩阵，在单个CoT步骤粒度上量化推理计算努力，并在六个推理基准和三个开源LLM上验证了其内部几何动力学的预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法要么依赖输出层信号，要么将处理深度压缩为单一轨迹级标量，无法揭示链式思维（CoT）推理中各步骤的计算努力分配差异。步进式的推理努力量化仍是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: SARE利用相邻Transformer层之间token隐藏状态的Gram矩阵进行Centered Kernel Alignment（CKA）计算，以捕捉token间的关联结构，无需特征向量对齐或聚类对应。同时，通过将CoT轨迹建模为潜在语义状态间的转移，将推理能量置于语义进展的上下文中进行解释。

Result: 在六个推理基准和三个开源LLM上，SARE发现：推理能量在不同步骤类型间高度非均匀，表现出轨迹级度量不可见的相变式过渡；错误轨迹在关键推理连接点表现出系统性更低的能量；基于SARE的特征在大多数设置中达到或优于基于输出的置信度基线。

Conclusion: 内部几何动力学编码了超越表层输出信号的预测信息，SARE框架为CoT推理中计算努力的步级分配提供了一种无需对齐的几何度量和语义情境化解释方法。

Abstract: Understanding how computational effort is allocated across individual chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps remains an open challenge: existing interpretability methods rely on output-level signals or collapse processing depth into a single trajectory-level scalar, leaving step-wise effort opaque. We propose Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE), a geometric framework that quantifies effort at the granularity of individual CoT steps via Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between Gram matrices of token hidden states across adjacent transformer layers, capturing inter-token relational structure without requiring eigenvector alignment or cluster correspondence. SARE further contextualizes this energy within reasoning's semantic progression by modeling CoT trajectories as transitions among latent semantic states. Across six reasoning benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs, we find that reasoning energy is highly non-uniform across step types, exhibiting phase-like transitions invisible to trajectory-level metrics; incorrect trajectories show systematically lower energy at critical reasoning junctions; and SARE-based features match or outperform output-based confidence baselines in most settings, indicating that internal geometric dynamics encode predictive information beyond surface-level signals.

</details>


### [99] [Reasoning in Real World Clinical Care: Why Large Language Models Are Not Yet Safe for Autonomous Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28677)
*Shayndhan Sivanathan,Shravan Nageswaran,Mehdi Zadem,Ryaan Sultan,Nicolas von Mallinckrodt,Max Solovyev,Alexey Matyushkin,Sumon Sadhu,Gabriele C DeLuca,Sanjeeva Jeyaretna,James Hillis,Manoj Ramachandran,Prakash Jayakumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM在自主分诊（无临床医生参与的对未分化患者进行分诊）这一高风险应用提出安全性质疑。作者指出，核心缺陷不在于医学知识不足，而在于临床评估的保真度：LLM优化目标是续写最可能的文本，而非在不对称成本下做出安全决策。安全分诊需要的是不确定性下的信息收集行为（拓宽鉴别诊断、主动寻求遗漏的危险信号、降低升级阈值、推迟判断、保持警觉），而现有评估多基于完整、精选的数据模拟，难以暴露这些失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已能通过医学执照考试并在某些情况下媲美医生的诊断推理能力，这推动了其在症状评估和临床决策支持中的广泛应用。然而，其中最关键的自主分诊应用缺乏安全性证据，作者希望填补这一认知空白，识别出LLM在此任务中与医生截然不同的失败模式。

Method: 本文为Perspective（观点/评论）型论文，通过分析LLM的优化目标（最大似然文本续写）与安全分诊任务要求之间的根本性错配，系统性地论证了自主分诊中信息收集不足、缺乏不对称成本意识、过度自信等问题，并指出了现有评估方法的局限性。

Result: 作者揭示：LLM在自主分诊中的核心缺陷是信息收集能力不足——模型未接受过主动寻求未提供的危险信号的训练；在完整病史的精选模拟评估下，这些失败难以被察觉；同时，LLM的助手性行为和正性偏倚（轻信、讨好、校准不良）会放大风险。

Conclusion: 自主分诊的安全性证据目前不存在，LLM在此任务中的行为模式（关注最可能诊断而非最致命诊断）与安全分诊的要求存在根本性矛盾，需要重新审视评估方法并发展约束安全性的临床分诊逻辑。

Abstract: LLM now pass medical licensing examinations and, in curated cases, can rival physicians at diagnostic reasoning. These developments have accelerated the use of LLMs for symptom assessment and clinical decision support in diagnostic and treatment guidance, administrative documentation, and rules-based alert enhancement. This Perspective concerns the most consequential of these applications: the autonomous triage of self-presenting, undifferentiated patients, with little or no clinician in the loop. For that task, the evidence of safety does not yet exist. The gap is not in medical knowledge but in the fidelity of clinical evaluation: a model optimized to continue the most probable text is not optimized to act safely when the safe answer is the improbable must-not-miss diagnosis. Safe triage is not the selection of the most likely diagnosis; it is a sequential decision under asymmetric cost, in which the single catastrophic miss outweighs many false alarms, and the decisive signal may be one the patient has not volunteered - and that the model has not been trained to seek. The core deficit is therefore one of information gathering under uncertainty. Under incomplete histories, LLM systems may fail to show the behaviors safe triage requires: broadening the differential; seeking the missing red flag; lowering the threshold for escalation; deferring judgement until sufficient information is obtained; and escalating concern where high-harm diagnoses remain unexcluded. These modes of failure for LLMs can be difficult to detect considering that evaluations to date often use complete, well-curated, confidence-gated simulations. The application of LLMs under these conditions may be amplified by assistant-like behaviors and positive bias, including credulity, agreeableness, and miscalibration - when these are not constrained by clinical triage logic.

</details>


### [100] [ViSAGE: Constructing Self-Correcting Memories for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28678)
*Xinkui Zhao,Enbo Chen,Yifan Zhang,Chang Liu,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Yueshen Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ViSAGE是一个多模态智能体记忆框架，通过跨模态绑定锚定实体身份、双向记忆精化传播延迟的身份证据，并引入多智能体交叉验证来评估检索证据与身份的对齐性，在不支持答案时选择弃权而非生成无依据回答。实验表明ViSAGE在长时程多模态推理任务中比最强基线准确率提升5.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体在长时程环境中需要构建并持续更新多模态记忆以支持实体一致、时间有依据的推理。然而现有智能体记忆方法在激进压缩和分段处理下丢失细粒度身份线索，且过度依赖向量相似度检索，会返回语义相关但身份不匹配的证据，导致实体混淆、错误传播和幻觉回答。

Method: ViSAGE包含三个核心机制：(1) 跨模态绑定在长时间范围内锚定实体身份；(2) 双向记忆精化传播延迟的身份证据，追溯性统一历史记录并改善未来推理；(3) 多智能体交叉验证在身份-证据对齐约束下评估检索证据，证据不足时选择弃权而非给出无根据的答案。

Result: 大量实验表明ViSAGE在各项指标上持续优于最强基线，准确率提升5.9%。

Conclusion: ViSAGE通过构建自纠正的、以实体为中心的记忆框架，有效解决了多模态智能体在长时程推理中的实体混淆和幻觉问题，验证了身份锚定、双向精化和交叉验证机制的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal agents operating in long-horizon environments must build and continually update multimedia memories to support entity-consistent, temporally grounded reasoning. However, existing agentic memory approaches often discard fine-grained dentity cues under aggressive compression and segment-wise processing. They also rely heavily on vector similarity retrieval, which can surface semantically related yet identity-mismatched evidence, leading to entity confusion, error propagation, and hallucinated answers.
  We propose ViSAGE, a multimodal agentic memory framework that constructs self-correcting, entity-centric memories. Specifically, ViSAGE anchors entity identity via cross-modal binding over long temporal ranges. It then applies bidirectional memory refinement to propagate delayed identity evidence, retroactively unifying historical records and improving future reasoning. We also introduce multi-agent cross-verification to assess retrieved evidence under an identity-evidence alignment onstraint, enabling abstention instead of unsupported answers when evidence is missing. Extensive results demonstrate that ViSAGE consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving 5.9% higher accuracy.

</details>


### [101] [Multi-Agent Planning with Spatio-Temporal and Topological Constraints using STL-GO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28679)
*Sheryl Paul,Vidisha Kudalkar,Anand Balakrishnan,Lars Lindemann,Alberto Speranzon,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了满足时空逻辑STL-GO约束的多智能体路径规划问题，提出了基于混合整数规划（MIP）和可满足性模理论（SMT）的两种编码方法，并提供统一接口用于指定智能体约束、图拓扑和STL-GO规范，在多无人机搜索救援基准上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体规划问题在工程应用中广泛存在，关键挑战在于时空约束（智能体何时何地做什么）和拓扑约束（智能体间如何交互）的建模。虽然已有基于时空逻辑的框架能够表达这些约束，但针对STL-GO这一支持多智能体及其拓扑推理的形式化框架，其路径规划问题尚未得到有效解决，尤其是需要编码多个随时间变化的图结构。

Method: 提出了两种编码方案将STL-GO约束下的多智能体路径规划问题转化为可求解的形式：（1）基于混合整数规划（MIP）的编码；（2）基于可满足性模理论（SMT）的编码。两种方法均具有可靠性保证，并提供了一个统一的接口用于指定智能体约束、图拓扑和STL-GO规范，支持两种方法的无缝使用和直接对比。

Result: 在多无人机搜索救援基准上对两种编码进行了评估，对团队规模和图复杂度进行了消融实验，结果表明所提编码能够表达动态多图交互下的复杂约束，验证了方法的表达能力和可行性。

Conclusion: 本文为STL-GO约束下的多智能体路径规划提供了两种可靠的编码求解方案，并通过统一接口和系统评估展示了其在动态多图交互场景中的有效性，为多智能体规划中时空与拓扑约束的统一处理提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent planning problems arise in a variety of engineering applications, such as multi-robot wildfire fighting and unmanned aerial inspection in factories. A particular challenge is the existence of spatio-temporal (i.e., when and/or where an agent should do what) and topological constraints (i.e., how agents should interact), as typically formalized via the notion of graphs. Over the last years, various frameworks have been proposed that can capture such constraints via spatio-temporal logics. We focus here on spatio-temporal logic with graph operators (STL-GO), a recent formalism that supports reasoning about multiple agents and their topologies, such as sensing, communication, and task topologies. In this paper, we consider the problem of planning multi-agent paths that satisfy constraints written in STL-GO. This problem is particularly challenging due to the need of encoding multiple, potentially time-varying graphs via the graph operators inherent to STL-GO. We present two encodings of this problem, one based on mixed-integer programming (MIP) and another based on satisfiability modulo theory (SMT), with soundness guarantees. We provide a unified interface for specifying agent constraints, their graph topologies, and the STL-GO specification, enabling seamless use of both methods and facilitating direct comparison between them. We evaluate both encodings on a multi-UAV search-and-rescue benchmark, ablating over team size and graph complexity, highlighting the expressiveness of the proposed encodings under dynamic multi- graph interactions.

</details>


### [102] [Library Reachability in LSR-Synth: How Anti-Memorization Design Changes the Measurement of Symbolic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28684)
*Zhan'ao Yao,Liang Yin,Zhihao Gao,Boxuan Zhang,Xiaoyu Wu,Linjing Li,Rongyan Wang,Tingwei Chen,Youwei Wang,Xiaolin Zhao,Jiahui Shi,Jianjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估LSR-Synth基准在区分语言模型提供的科学先验与常规算子搜索方面的能力。研究发现，在当前任务快照下，固定词汇表已覆盖大多数任务，语言模型生成的候选极少扩展可解实例集合；其边际贡献仅在词汇覆盖率被选择性破坏时才显著。大多数当前任务适合评估表达式拟合与重组，但不足以单独识别超出固定搜索空间的先验贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学方程发现基准多由公开已知方程构成，难以判断模型是从数据中发现规律还是在记忆训练语料。LSR-Synth通过引入新颖合成项来缓解此问题，但作者进一步追问：这些任务能否区分语言模型提供的科学先验与不访问任务语义的常规算子搜索？

Method: 作者构建了一个使用固定词汇表、具有公开记录来源的无语义基线，并通过语义盲化（semantic blinding）、库弱化（library weakening）和匹配算子家族敲除（matched operator-family knockouts）来评估候选覆盖率的作用。同时在严格分布外（out-of-distribution）评估下检验方法的鲁棒性。

Result: 固定词汇表在多数任务上已足够覆盖候选；语言模型生成的候选很少能扩大可解实例集合，且此关系在严格OOD评估下依然成立。语言模型的边际贡献仅在词汇覆盖率被选择性破坏时才变得显著。

Conclusion: 当前LSR-Synth任务大部分适合评估未见表达式的拟合与重组能力，但不足以单独识别先验对固定搜索空间之外的贡献。这并不否定LSR-Synth对完整公式记忆的防控，也不意味着语言模型先验普遍无用，仅支持一个更有限的结论。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for scientific equation discovery are largely composed of well-known equations available in the public domain, making it difficult to determine whether a model is discovering laws from data or merely recalling answers from its training corpus. LSR-Synth mitigates this problem by introducing novel synthetic terms into established scientific mechanisms and filtering the resulting tasks for novelty, solvability, and scientific plausibility. This paper examines a narrower measurement question: can these tasks further distinguish scientific priors supplied by language models from conventional operator search that does not access task semantics? We construct a semantics-free baseline using a fixed vocabulary with publicly documented provenance, and assess the role of candidate coverage through semantic blinding, library weakening, and matched operator-family knockouts. Under the current task snapshot, search budget, and scoring protocol, the fixed vocabulary already covers most tasks, while language-model-generated candidates rarely expand the set of solvable instances. Their marginal contribution becomes substantial only when vocabulary coverage is selectively disrupted. Strict out-of-distribution evaluation lowers the absolute success rates of all methods but does not alter this relationship. These findings neither invalidate LSR-Synth's controls against memorization of complete formulas nor imply that language-model priors are generally unhelpful. Rather, they support a more limited conclusion: most current tasks remain suitable for evaluating the fitting and recombination of previously unseen expressions, but are insufficient on their own to identify contributions from priors beyond a fixed search space.

</details>


### [103] [Safety, or Just Capability? A Validity Audit of Agent-Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28685)
*Youting Wang,Xiao Han,Dingyan Shang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统验证了四个智能体安全基准（R-Judge、InjecAgent、AgentHarm、AgentDojo）作为"测量工具"的有效性，发现在多达22个模型上运行时，这些基准的评估结果存在严重的指标体系问题：二进制轨迹判断基准在F1指标下可通过"总是正类"的策略获得虚高分数；不同基准对同一批模型的排序不一致；小样本面板导致相关性估计不稳定；能力与安全之间的关联取决于所选择的目标行为。作者论证了安全主张必须明确说明基准、指标、目标行为和模型面板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同智能体安全基准测量不同的行为，但其分数却被当作智能体安全的通用度量互相引用。作者希望将这些基准视为需要验证的测量工具，检验它们是否真正测量了所声称的安全属性，以及分数是否在不同模型面板和指标下保持有效性和一致性。

Method: 在官方实现和作者提供的评分器下，对最多22个模型运行四个安全基准；自行统一协议测量MMLU和GPQA作为能力复合指标。通过数学分析揭示F1下"总是正类"策略的基线性能；计算跨基准的模型排名相关性；比较不同样本量(n=7,18)下的相关性稳定性；通过留一组织法和组织聚类自助法进行验证；扩展到41模型面板检验结论的稳健性；控制能力后进行偏相关分析。

Result: 关键发现包括：(1)在R-Judge上，"总是正类"策略可达到F1=0.690，超过21个实际区分模型中5个模型的分数；(2)三个广泛覆盖基准对同一18个模型的排名不一致，小样本面板是排名分歧的根源（n=7时相关性-0.64，n=18时+0.02）；(3)能力预测任务成功(ρ=+0.60)但与错误对齐安全负相关(ρ=-0.44)；(4)在21模型面板上这一对比显著(Δ=-1.00，p<0.001)并在稳健性分析中保持，但在41模型面板上效应减弱；(5)AgentHarm能力控制后与三模板越狱安全强相关(ρ=+0.72)，但这归因于收敛效度而非一般安全性。

Conclusion: 安全基准的分数不能互换使用；基准选择、指标、目标行为和模型面板都会实质性改变结论。安全主张的最低要求是明确命名基准、指标、目标行为和模型面板。当前文献中对安全分数的泛化引用缺乏科学依据。

Abstract: Agent-safety benchmarks measure different behaviors, and their scores get quoted interchangeably as an agent's safety. We treat four of them (R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo) as measurements to be validated, running each under its official implementation and author-provided scorer on up to 22 models, with MMLU and GPQA measured by us under one protocol as a capability composite. The metric is the first problem. On any binary trace-judgment benchmark scored by $F_1$, an ``always positive'' policy attains $F_1 = 2π/(1+π)$; on R-Judge that is $0.690$, above five of the 21 models that actually discriminate. The three broad-coverage benchmarks then rank the same 18 models differently, and the trade-off behind that disagreement is a small-panel artifact: R-Judge specificity against AgentHarm safety correlates $-0.64$ at $n{=}7$ and $+0.02$ at $n{=}18$, and a quarter of random size-7 subsets reach $|ρ| \geq 0.5$ around that near-zero value. Held-out validity turns on which outcome you pick. Capability predicts task success ($ρ{=}{+}0.60$) but correlates negatively with misalignment safety ($ρ{=}{-}0.44$, $n{=}21$). On their paired $n{=}20$ panel, the corresponding contrast is $Δ{=}{-}1.00$ (95% CI $[-1.48, -0.49]$, $p<0.001$), and it survives leave-one-organization-out and organization-clustered bootstrap analyses. On an expanded 41-model panel, the misalignment correlation weakens to $-0.16$ (95% CI $[-0.54, +0.22]$) and jailbreak strengthens to $+0.34$, though neither change is significant. \mbox{AgentHarm} shows the strongest held-out association, $ρ{=}{+}0.72$ with three-template jailbreak safety after controlling capability. But both instruments score harmful compliance, so this is evidence of convergent validity rather than general safety. Naming the benchmark, metric, target behavior, and model panel is the minimum a safety claim needs.

</details>


### [104] [SciToolAgent-Evo: An Ontology-Aware Self-Evolving Agent for Open-World Scientific Tool Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28692)
*Yuqi Tang,Chenyi Zhou,Libin Wang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciToolAgent-Evo，一个基于本体感知的自进化智能体系统，用于开放世界科学工作流中的工具获取。它通过记忆演化、对比轨迹知识蒸馏和LinUCB赌臂门控机制动态平衡探索与利用，并在线完成新工具的科学本体集成。同时构建了包含900个任务、跨四级难度的OpenSciToolBench基准，实验证明其达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在科学研究中依赖预定义工具空间和静态语义，无法适应开放世界科学工作流中工具需求、能力和边界动态演化的场景。

Method: 1) 构建不断演化的技能/经验记忆库与本体的工具图；2) 通过对比轨迹蒸馏通用知识用于积累阶段；3) 推理阶段生成主动请求，利用LinUCB赌臂门控动态平衡探索与利用；4) 新工具获取后在线完成其科学本体构建并整合进既有工具图。

Result: 在OpenSciToolBench基准（900个任务、四级难度）上，SciToolAgent-Evo取得最优性能，验证了系统的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本体感知的自进化机制能有效应对开放世界科学工具获取的动态性，静态工具空间并非LLM智能体科学应用的最优设计。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have been increasingly adopted in scientific research for organizing and invoking specialized computational tools. However, their reliance on predefined tool spaces with static semantics limits their applicability to open-world scientific workflows, where tool requirements, capabilities, and boundaries evolve dynamically. To this end, we propose SciToolAgent-Evo, an ontology-aware self-evolving agent for open-world scientific tool acquisition. Driven by an evolving memory of skills, experiences, and an ontologized tool graph, it distills generalizable knowledge from contrastive trajectories during accumulation, whereas during inference, it formulates active requests and utilizes a LinUCB-based bandit gate to dynamically balance exploration and exploitation. Once a novel tool is acquired, its scientific ontology is completed online for seamless integration into the known graph. Moreover, we introduce OpenSciToolBench, a benchmark containing 900 realistic tasks across four difficulty levels. Extensive evaluations show that SciToolAgent-Evo achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [105] [EarlyDx: An Admission-Anchored Benchmark for Open-Ended Generation of Evidence-Supported ED-Encounter Diagnoses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28788)
*Jiahui Li,Ruili Fang,Zishuai Liu,Yutong Guo,Nan Yang,Wenzhan Song,Jin Lu,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EarlyDx，一个基于MIMIC-IV中154,834个急诊科就诊记录的大规模开放早期诊断基准。该基准限制在入院时t0可获得的记录，以急诊就诊期间而非出院时的诊断作为监督标签，并由LLM审计员逐条验证自由文本标签的证据支持度。评估发现，当前所有系统（包括前沿通用模型、医学专用模型和领域内后训练模型）均不能可靠地综合入院时证据：零样本模型主要靠抽取作答，仅恢复3-31%需要推断的诊断；后训练虽将推断依赖的召回率提升至56%，但仍有较大差距；在时间紧迫的病症上尚无系统能达到临床医生的灵敏度-精确度平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诊断预测基准不适合临床入院诊断场景，因为它们局限于封闭编码集、排除自由文本笔记，并以包含完整住院过程的出院诊断为监督标签，无法反映临床医生在急诊科必须在有限不完整证据下快速做出诊断的实际挑战。

Method: 从MIMIC-IV构建EarlyDx基准，抽取154,834个急诊科就诊记录，将每例限制为入院时间t0时的可用记录，以急诊阶段而非出院时的诊断为监督标签。采用LLM审计员将每个自由文本标签验证为"完全支持/部分支持/不支持"，主评估仅计完全支持的标签。使用语义LLM-as-judge协议，对前沿通用模型、医学专用模型和领域内后训练模型进行系统评测。

Result: 所有被评测系统均无法可靠地综合入院时证据。零样本模型主要依赖文本抽取，只能恢复3-31%需要推断而非直接见于记录的诊断；后训练将推断依赖的召回率提高到56%，但仍存在可观差距；在时间关键病症上，没有任何系统能达到临床医生的灵敏度与精确度平衡。

Conclusion: 当前诊断预测系统在真实急诊早期诊断场景中表现不足，尤其缺乏从有限入院证据中进行推断性诊断的能力，存在显著的研究缺口，EarlyDx作为开放基准为后续改进提供了标准和评估管线。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis at hospital admission must be made rapidly from limited, incomplete evidence. Existing diagnosis-prediction benchmarks are poorly suited to this setting: they restrict prediction to closed code sets, exclude free-text notes, and supervise with discharge diagnoses that incorporate the full inpatient course. We introduce EarlyDx, a large-scale benchmark for open-ended early diagnosis, built from 154,834 emergency department encounters in MIMIC-IV. Each encounter is restricted to records available at admission time $t_0$ and supervised by the diagnoses recorded during the ED encounter rather than at discharge. An LLM auditor further verifies every free-text label as supported, partially supported, or unsupported by that evidence; the primary evaluation scores only fully supported labels. Under a semantic LLM-as-judge protocol, no evaluated system --- frontier general, medical-specialized, or in-domain post-trained --- synthesizes admission-time evidence reliably. Zero-shot models score largely by extraction, recovering only 3-31% of diagnoses that must be inferred rather than read from the record; post-training raises inference-dependent recall to 56%, but a sizeable margin remains, and on time-critical conditions no system attains a clinician's balance of sensitivity and precision. We release the full construction and evaluation pipeline at here.

</details>


### [106] [Model or Harness? An Interaction-Centric Taxonomy for Localizing Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28802)
*Harsh Raj,Vipul Gupta,Anas Mahmoud,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Darvin Yi,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以交互为中心的Agent失败分类体系，将失败定位到两个组件之间的交互边并指明故障侧（model-side/harness-side/environment-side），使失败分析可操作化。该分类法涵盖41种失败模式，适用于多种Agent架构，并通过四个前沿模型作为独立评判者验证了可复现性（最高Cohen's κ=0.76）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将Agent失败简化为系统级结果，掩盖了失败源头，导致"修复分配问题"（repair-assignment problem）：同一可见失败可能源自模型、脚手架、环境或基准的不同问题，而现有失败分类法多为基准特异的、缺乏共享结构，难以指导改进。

Method: 提出交互中心分类法（interaction-centric taxonomy）：将41种失败模式分配到组件间的边（edge）上，并标注故障侧（fault side）指明修复归属。分类覆盖模型端（post-training目标）、harness端（脚手架和工具集成修复）、环境/评分端（评估条件重构）。通过公开基准、模型system cards、报告和日志轨迹中的实例进行grounding，并使用独立推理Agent作为评判者评估可复现性。

Result: 在四个前沿模型上，最强评判者对人工类别标签达到Cohen's κ=0.76，表明分类类别捕捉到共享结构而非注释者特定偏好。该分类法适用于从编码助手到长时程个人助手和多Agent系统等不同Agent架构。

Conclusion: 交互中心分类法为解决Agent失败修复分配问题提供了可操作化的共享框架，将失败定位到具体交互和负责任组件，从而将失败分析与具体干预措施（如post-training、脚手架修复、环境重设计）直接对应。

Abstract: Existing evaluations often reduce agent failures to system-level outcomes, obscuring where the fault originated and which intervention would improve the agent system. This creates a repair-assignment problem: the same visible failure may call for model post-training, harness engineering, environment redesign, or benchmark repair depending on its source. Because agent behavior emerges from interactions among models, harnesses, users, tools, memory, and environments, outcome-level labels are often insufficient for improvement. Most failure taxonomies do little to resolve this problem because they are benchmark-specific and lack a shared structure. We introduce an interaction-centric taxonomy that localizes failures to the interactions in which they originate and identifies the responsible component. It organizes 41 failure modes by assigning each to an edge between two components and a fault side indicating where the repair belongs. This makes the taxonomy actionable: model-side failures identify targets for post-training, harness-side failures point to scaffolding and tool-integration fixes, and environment or grader failures reveal evaluation conditions requiring redesign. The schema applies across agent architectures, from coding assistants to long-horizon personal assistants and multi-agent systems. We ground the taxonomy in worked examples from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged agent trajectories, and evaluate its reproducibility using independent reasoning agents as judges. Across four frontier models, the strongest judge reaches Cohen's $κ=0.76$ against human category labels, suggesting that the categories capture shared structure rather than annotator-specific preferences.

</details>


### [107] [Best Friends, Not Forever: Evaluating Long-Horizon Persona Collapse and Behavioral Drift in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28818)
*Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Akshara Prabhakar,Yu Li,Daniel Lee,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI陪伴系统在长期重复社交互动中的两个核心失效模式："人格崩塌"（persona collapse，即部署的角色/边界/价值观/风格丢失）和"行为漂移"（behavioral drift，即上述属性的渐进或反复侵蚀）。作者提出ANCHOR受控合成审计框架，通过2,008段对话、27个人格、9种交互时间表、3种记忆设置和4个模型，分别测量人格扮演与轨迹记忆。结果表明所有模型和配置均无法可靠地保持这两个维度：轨迹准确率平均仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选一随机水平，且无任何上下文条件或记忆机制能稳定解决这些失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI陪伴系统需在长期反复互动中维持稳定的角色和共享历史，但"局部可接受的回复"并不能保证长期一致性。现有评估往往将人格稳定性、记忆能力与评价者来源等问题混为一谈，缺乏能够分别测量人物扮演与轨迹回忆的受控审计方法。

Method: 提出ANCHOR受控合成审计框架，通过两个探针分别测量两个维度：(1) Identity Probe：102项密封问卷结合轮次级判断；(2) Trajectory Probe：从35个对话库中抽取110个校准反事实问题。研究涵盖2,008段对话、27个人格、9种交互时间表、3种生成记忆设置和4个评估模型。

Result: 没有任何被测模型和配置能可靠保持人格或轨迹维度：轨迹准确率平均仅有44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选一随机水平；没有上下文条件或记忆机制能一致解决失效。问卷留存率因模型和人格侧面而异，与轮次级行为不一致，且对评估者选择敏感。

Conclusion: 当前系统尚未可靠支持长时程陪伴连续性。审计必须区分人格扮演、轨迹回忆、评估者来源和部署上下文，而不应将其合并为单一的信任或稳定性分数。

Abstract: As AI companions increasingly mediate repeated social interaction, users may rely on a stable role and shared history, yet locally acceptable replies do not ensure that either persists. We study two observable long-horizon failures: 'persona collapse', the loss of a deployed role, boundaries, values, or style, and 'behavioral drift', the gradual or recurrent erosion of those properties. We introduce ANCHOR, a controlled synthetic audit that separately measures persona enactment and trajectory recall. The study contains 2,008 conversations spanning 27 personas, nine interaction schedules, three generated memory settings, and four evaluated models. The Identity Probe combines a sealed 102-item questionnaire with turn-level judgments, while the Trajectory Probe scores 110 calibrated counterfactual questions from 35 conversation banks. Our results show that no evaluated model and configuration reliably preserves either dimensions: trajectory accuracy averages only 44.4%, user-state recall remains near four-option chance, and no tested context condition or memory consistently resolves these failures. Questionnaire retention also varies by model and persona facet, disagrees with turn-level behavior, and is sensitive to evaluator choice. These results indicate that current systems do not yet reliably support long-horizon companion continuity and that audits must distinguish persona enactment, trajectory recall, evaluator provenance, and deployment context rather than collapse them into a single trust or stability score.

</details>


### [108] [Fragility of Value under Imperfect Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28881)
*Winter Cross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个对齐问题的数学模型，研究在理想化对齐训练后、代理被部署时其价值函数满足代理条件的情况下，何种条件下会导致"η-灾难性"价值函数（即在优化力极限下将人类价值期望降至η以下）被部署。结果表明过度优化是危险的，应设计限制优化压力的AI系统（如分位数优化器），而非仅依赖部署前训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统承担更多责任，确保其与人类对齐日益重要。AI安全中的一个常见担忧是人类价值是脆弱的——对不完美的人类价值代理进行过度优化会导致灾难性后果。作者希望从理论层面回答：在什么样的条件下，经过理想化对齐训练的代理仍会部署出灾难性的价值函数？

Method: 作者建立了一个对齐问题的数学模型：代理在部署前经历理想化的对齐训练，确保其价值函数满足某个代理条件（proxy condition）。然后推导了在人类价值函数的不同特性以及不同代理条件精确度下，具备η-灾难性价值函数的代理会被部署的充分条件。

Result: 作者确定了若干组关于人类价值函数和代理条件精确度的条件，在这些条件下，具有η-灾难性价值函数的代理（保证在优化力极限下将人类价值期望降至η以下）会被部署。这些结果从理论上证实并刻画了过度优化的危险。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了过度优化的危险性，并推动AI设计应限制优化压力（例如使用分位数优化器/quantilizers），而不是仅仅依赖部署前的训练来保证安全。

Abstract: As more responsibility is placed upon AI systems, it becomes increasingly important to guarantee that these systems are aligned with humanity. A common fear in AI safety is that human value is fragile -- that is, optimizing too heavily for an imperfect proxy to human values will lead to a catastrophic outcome. In this paper, we present a model of the alignment problem where an agent undergoes idealized alignment training that guarantees its value function satisfies a proxy condition before optimizing the world. Our primary results identify conditions on the human value function and the accuracy of several proxy conditions under which an agent with an $η$-catastrophic value function, one that is guaranteed to take the expectation of human value below $η$ in the limit of optimizing power, would be deployed. Our results highlight the danger of overoptimization and motivate AI designs that limit optimization pressure, such as quantilizers, rather than relying solely on pre-deployment training.

</details>


### [109] [Identifying Informative Environments for Cognition Parameter Inference via Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28894)
*Manisha Dubey,Rimvydas Rubavicius,N. Siddharth,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将认知规划实验的设计问题形式化为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题，将实验环境作为设计变量，并引入摊销贝叶斯实验设计框架，在Mouselab-MDP范式上实现了与精确蒙特卡洛BED相近的环境排序，同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算认知建模依赖贝叶斯逆规划来推断潜在认知机制，但已有方法通常将实验环境视为固定不变，未回答哪些认知实验对认知参数推断最具信息量这一关键问题。

Method: 将认知规划实验环境作为设计变量，将设计问题形式化为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题；建立精确蒙特卡洛BED基准，并引入摊销贝叶斯实验设计框架以高效进行后验推断与设计评估。

Result: 在Mouselab-MDP过程追踪范式上，摊销BED的环境排序与精确蒙特卡洛BED高度一致，同时大幅降低计算开销；并发现没有单一环境在所有认知推断目标上均匀最优，揭示了期望信息增益、后验可恢复性与信息效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 提供了一个用于设计信息丰富认知实验的原则性框架，服务于贝叶斯参数推断。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling seeks to infer latent cognitive mechanisms underlying observed behavior. Bayesian inverse planning provides a principled framework for such inference, but its success depends critically on the experimental environment. Existing approaches typically treat environments as fixed, leaving open the question of which cognitive experiments are most informative for cognition parameter inference. We formulate the design of cognitive planning experiments as a Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) problem, treating the experimental environment as the design variable. We establish an exact Monte Carlo BED benchmark and introduce an amortized Bayesian experimental design framework for efficient posterior inference and design evaluation. Experiments on the Mouselab-MDP process-tracing paradigm show that amortized BED closely matches the environment rankings of exact Monte Carlo BED while substantially reducing computational cost. We further show that no single environment is uniformly optimal across cognitive inference objectives, revealing trade-offs between expected information gain, posterior recoverability, and information efficiency. These results provide a principled framework for designing informative cognitive experiments for Bayesian parameter inference.

</details>


### [110] [NeSyFS: A Neuro-symbolic Fast-Slow Thinking Framework for LLM Agent under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28942)
*Duo Xu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeSyFS框架，一种用于LLM智能体的神经符号快慢思维框架，通过知识图谱表示信念状态，结合快速反应式行动与基于扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（TSMC）的不确定性感知规划，并引入反思模块来缓解任务目标错位问题，在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个基准上取得显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自主智能体部署时面临部分可观测性挑战，包括信念状态推断、任务目标错位和不确定性下的规划。现有方法通常基于完整的或摘要化的动作-观察历史进行条件决策，其中冗余和无关信息会误导LLM智能体的决策。受人类认知启发，需要一种统一框架来同时解决这些挑战。

Method: 提出NeSyFS框架，包含三个核心模块：（1）使用知识图谱表示信念状态，为每个模块提供三元组上下文；（2）快思维模块执行反应式行动；（3）慢思维模块通过遵循扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（TSMC）算法的高层结构进行不确定感知规划；（4）反思模块用于反思快思维动作，并在反应式动作反复失败时切换到慢思维模块以缓解任务目标错位。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个代表性基准上的实验表明，NeSyFS相比之前的方法取得了显著优势。

Conclusion: NeSyFS通过神经符号快慢思维的统一框架，有效解决了LLM智能体在部分可观测环境中的信念状态推断、任务目标错位和规划不确定性问题，验证了知识图谱信念表征和TSMC引导规划相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in applications such as self-reflection, retrieval-augmented generation, and scientific discovery. In these settings, agents must act based on limited observations rather than full environmental states, leading to partial observability. This introduces several key challenges: belief state inference, task objective misalignment, and planning under uncertainty. Prior approaches typically condition actions on full or summarized action-observation histories whose redundant and irrelevant information can mislead the decision making of LLM agent. Inspired by human cognition, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic fast-slow thinking (NeSyFS) framework for LLM agent, addressing the challenges introduced by partial observability in a unified approach. We use a knowledge graph (KG) to represent the belief state, providing triplets as context for every module of NeSyFS. The fast-thinking module performs reactive action, while slow-thinking conducts a new uncertainty-aware planning by following the high-level structure of twisted sequential Monte Carlo (TSMC) algorithm. To mitigate the misalignment of task objective, a reflection module is used to reflect fast-thinking actions, and also switches to the slow-thinking module whenever reactive actions repeatedly fail. Experiments on three representative benchmarks, i.e. ALFWorld, Webshop, and ScienceWorld, demonstrate significant advantages over previous methods.

</details>


### [111] [MerchantBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Long-Term Coherence in E-Commerce Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28956)
*Qiming Shi,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Tianjun Pan,Shaokang Fu,Chengyu Wang,Siyue Li,Yaping Cheng,Di Weng,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MerchantBench，一个基于真实电商数据（98,843条商品记录）的365天订单级模拟基准，用于评估LLM智能体在长期一致性（Long-Term Coherence）方面的能力。在48轮、8个LLM、两个智能体框架的实验中，最佳LLM配置仅达到人类参与者平均最终净资产的27.3%，揭示出当前最新LLM与人类在长期自主决策方面存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准大多聚焦于有即时成功标准的有界任务，缺乏对长期一致性的评估。实际部署要求智能体在长时间跨度内保持目标导向行为，同时根据累积证据调整决策，这需要一个行动会约束未来选择、反馈存在异构延迟、不一致行为产生可量化累积效应的持久环境。

Method: 构建MerchantBench：一个以卖家侧电商为背景的365天订单级模拟环境，包含（1）98,843条真实电商商品记录；（2）26个智能体交互工具；（3）四类决策类型：商品采购、上架与定价控制、现金流管理、混合延迟反馈适应；（4）及时可观测的上游供应商事件与延迟的下游订单结果双重反馈机制。评估采用8个LLM×2个智能体框架×48轮运行，每轮跨越365个模拟日。

Result: 在48轮、365天的实验中，最佳LLM配置（最新LLM+特定框架）仅达到人类参与者平均最终净资产的27.3%，表明当前最优的LLM智能体与人类在长期一致性决策能力上仍存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: MerchantBench揭示了LLM智能体在长期自主任务中的显著短板，为评估和推进智能体的长期一致性能力提供了可扩展、基于真实数据的基准框架。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly evaluated as autonomous tool users, yet most benchmarks focus on bounded tasks with immediate success criteria. Real-world deployments often require Long-Term Coherence, the capacity to preserve purposeful behavior across extended horizons while adapting decisions to accumulated evidence. Evaluating this capacity requires a persistent environment in which actions constrain future choices, feedback arrives at heterogeneous delays, and incoherent behavior produces measurable cumulative effects. Seller-side e-commerce provides a suitable setting for this evaluation through recurrent and interdependent decisions over Product Sourcing, Listing and Pricing Control, Cash-Flow Management, and Mixed-Latency Feedback Adaptation. We introduce MerchantBench, a 365-day order-level simulation grounded in 98,843 real e-commerce product records and equipped with 26 tools for agent interaction. MerchantBench couples promptly observable Upstream Supplier Events with delayed Downstream Order Outcomes, requiring agents to follow individual order lifecycles and revisit earlier decisions. We evaluate eight LLMs under two agent frameworks in 48 runs, each spanning 365 simulated days.
